# Copied the critical vanilla effects that are only defined in combat events
# Scripted effects not defined in common/scripted effects must be defined in each file they are used

adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
	# Adjust scope:sc_attacker's scores if we're looking at them.
	if = {
		limit = { 
			this = scope:sc_attacker 
		}
		if = {
			limit = { 
				has_variable = sc_attacker_injury_risk_score 
				POD_damage_spirit_custom_trigger = {
					RECIPIENT = scope:sc_attacker
					ACTOR = scope:sc_defender
				}
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_attacker_injury_risk_score
				add = $INJURY_RISK$
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { 
				has_variable = sc_attacker_duel_success_score 
				POD_damage_spirit_custom_trigger = {
					RECIPIENT = scope:sc_defender
					ACTOR = scope:sc_attacker
				}
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_attacker_duel_success_score
				add = $DUEL_SUCCESS$
			}
		}
	}

	# Adjust scope:sc_defender's scores if we're looking at them.
	if = {
		limit = { 
			this = scope:sc_defender
		}
		if = {
			limit = { 
				has_variable = sc_defender_injury_risk_score 
				POD_damage_spirit_custom_trigger = {
					RECIPIENT = scope:sc_defender
					ACTOR = scope:sc_attacker
				}
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_defender_injury_risk_score
				add = $INJURY_RISK$
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { 
				has_variable = sc_defender_duel_success_score 
				POD_damage_spirit_custom_trigger = {
					RECIPIENT = scope:sc_attacker
					ACTOR = scope:sc_defender
				}
			}
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_defender_duel_success_score
				add = $DUEL_SUCCESS$
			}
		}
	}
	
	# CfV PoD: tooltip if you can't damage spirits
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					this = scope:sc_defender
					NOT = {
						POD_damage_spirit_custom_trigger = {
							RECIPIENT = scope:sc_attacker
							ACTOR = scope:sc_defender
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					this = scope:sc_attacker
					NOT = {
						POD_damage_spirit_custom_trigger = {
							RECIPIENT = scope:sc_defender
							ACTOR = scope:sc_attacker
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		custom_description_no_bullet = { text = POD_duel_cant_damage_spirit }
	}

	# Inform the current scope about how this will impact duel_success_score.
	if = {
		limit = { 
			OR = {
				$DUEL_SUCCESS$ = duel_success_none
				AND = {
					this = scope:sc_defender
					NOT = {
						POD_damage_spirit_custom_trigger = {
							RECIPIENT = scope:sc_attacker
							ACTOR = scope:sc_defender
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					this = scope:sc_attacker
					NOT = {
						POD_damage_spirit_custom_trigger = {
							RECIPIENT = scope:sc_defender
							ACTOR = scope:sc_attacker
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		# If this is a past tense tooltip, refer to scope:my_foe grammatically.
		if = {
			limit = { this = scope:my_foe }
			custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.duel_success.none.has_happened
		}
		# Otherwise, we do it from first person.
		else = { custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.duel_success.none }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { $DUEL_SUCCESS$ = duel_success_low }
		# If this is a past tense tooltip, refer to scope:my_foe grammatically.
		if = {
			limit = { this = scope:my_foe }
			custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.duel_success.low.has_happened
		}
		# Otherwise, we do it from first person.
		else = { custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.duel_success.low }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { $DUEL_SUCCESS$ = duel_success_medium }
		# If this is a past tense tooltip, refer to scope:my_foe grammatically.
		if = {
			limit = { this = scope:my_foe }
			custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.duel_success.medium.has_happened
		}
		# Otherwise, we do it from first person.
		else = { custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.duel_success.medium }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { $DUEL_SUCCESS$ = duel_success_high }
		# If this is a past tense tooltip, refer to scope:my_foe grammatically.
		if = {
			limit = { this = scope:my_foe }
			custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.duel_success.high.has_happened
		}
		# Otherwise, we do it from first person.
		else = { custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.duel_success.high }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { $DUEL_SUCCESS$ = duel_success_very_high }
		# If this is a past tense tooltip, refer to scope:my_foe grammatically.
		if = {
			limit = { this = scope:my_foe }
			custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.duel_success.very_high.has_happened
		}
		# Otherwise, we do it from first person.
		else = { custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.duel_success.very_high }
	}

	# Aaaand finally tell current scope how this'll impact injury_risk_score.
	if = {
		limit = { 
			OR = {
				$INJURY_RISK$ = injury_risk_none
				AND = {
					this = scope:sc_defender
					NOT = {
						POD_damage_spirit_custom_trigger = {
							RECIPIENT = scope:sc_defender
							ACTOR = scope:sc_attacker
						}
					}
				}
				AND = {
					this = scope:sc_attacker
					NOT = {
						POD_damage_spirit_custom_trigger = {
							RECIPIENT = scope:sc_attacker
							ACTOR = scope:sc_defender
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		# If this is a past tense tooltip, refer to scope:my_foe grammatically.
		if = {
			limit = { this = scope:my_foe }
			custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.injury.none.has_happened
		}
		# Otherwise, we do it from first person.
		else = { custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.injury.none }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { $INJURY_RISK$ = injury_risk_low }
		# If this is a past tense tooltip, refer to scope:my_foe grammatically.
		if = {
			limit = { this = scope:my_foe }
			custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.injury.low.has_happened
		}
		# Otherwise, we do it from first person.
		else = { custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.injury.low }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { $INJURY_RISK$ = injury_risk_medium }
		# If this is a past tense tooltip, refer to scope:my_foe grammatically.
		if = {
			limit = { this = scope:my_foe }
			custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.injury.medium.has_happened
		}
		# Otherwise, we do it from first person.
		else = { custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.injury.medium }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { $INJURY_RISK$ = injury_risk_high }
		# If this is a past tense tooltip, refer to scope:my_foe grammatically.
		if = {
			limit = { this = scope:my_foe }
			custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.injury.high.has_happened
		}
		# Otherwise, we do it from first person.
		else = { custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.injury.high }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { $INJURY_RISK$ = injury_risk_very_high }
		# If this is a past tense tooltip, refer to scope:my_foe grammatically.
		if = {
			limit = { this = scope:my_foe }
			custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.injury.very_high.has_happened
		}
		# Otherwise, we do it from first person.
		else = { custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.injury.very_high }
	}
}

# Add positive prowess character modifiers from the current move.
increment_combat_buffs_effect = {
	# Determine what our current level of combat buff is.
	## Hidden effect for easy folding & readability.
	hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_01_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_02_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_03_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_04_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_05_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_06_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_07_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_08_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_09_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_10_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_11_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_12_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_13_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_14_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_15_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_16_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_17_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_18_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_19_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_20_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_21_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_22_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_23_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_24_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_25_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_26_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_27_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_28_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_29_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_30_modifier
					}
				}
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 0
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_01_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 1
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_02_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 2
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_03_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 3
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_04_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 4
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_05_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 5
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_06_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 6
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_07_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 7
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_08_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 8
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_09_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 9
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_10_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 10
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_11_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 11
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_12_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 12
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_13_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 13
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_14_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 14
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_15_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 15
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_16_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 16
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_17_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 17
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_18_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 18
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_19_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 19
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_20_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 20
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_21_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 21
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_22_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 22
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_23_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 23
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_24_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 24
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_25_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 25
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_26_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 26
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_27_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 27
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_28_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 28
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_29_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 29
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_30_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_buff
					value = 30
				}
				#Show a tooltip in case the character is overflowing at max buff.
				custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.tt.maximum_buff_reached
			}
	}
	# Grab the amount we want to add and change the combat modifier accordingly.
	change_local_variable = {
		name = current_duel_edge_buff
		add = $BUFF_INCREMENT$
	}
	# Remove any existing duel edge buffs.
	hidden_effect = { remove_temporary_duel_edge_buff_modifiers_effect = yes }
	# Add the buff corresponding to our new level of edge.
	if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 1 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_01_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 2 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_02_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 3 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_03_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 4 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_04_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 5 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_05_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 6 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_06_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 7 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_07_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 8 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_08_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 9 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_09_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 10 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_10_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 11 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_11_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 12 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_12_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 13 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_13_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 14 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_14_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 15 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_15_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 16 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_16_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 17 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_17_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 18 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_18_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 19 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_19_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 20 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_20_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 21 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_21_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 22 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_22_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 23 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_23_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 24 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_24_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 25 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_25_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 26 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_26_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 27 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_27_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 28 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_28_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff = 29 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_29_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_buff >= 30 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_buff_30_modifier
		#Show a tooltip in case the character is overflowing at max buff.
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.tt.maximum_buff_reached
	}
	# Finally, note that the character needs a stats recalc.
	add_character_flag = sce_needs_forced_recalc_flag
}

# Add negative prowess character modifiers from the current move.
increment_combat_debuffs_effect = {
	# Determine what our current level of combat buff is.
	## Hidden effect for easy folding & readability.
	hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_01_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_02_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_03_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_04_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_05_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_06_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_07_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_08_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_09_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_10_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_11_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_12_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_13_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_14_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_15_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_16_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_17_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_18_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_19_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_20_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_21_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_22_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_23_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_24_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_25_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_26_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_27_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_28_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_29_modifier
						has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_30_modifier
					}
				}
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 0
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_01_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 1
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_02_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 2
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_03_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 3
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_04_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 4
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_05_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 5
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_06_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 6
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_07_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 7
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_08_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 8
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_09_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 9
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_10_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 10
				}
			}

				set_local_variable = {

				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 13

				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 17
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_18_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 18
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_19_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 19
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_20_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 20
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_21_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 21
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_22_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 22
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_23_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 23
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_24_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {

					value = 24
				}

			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_25_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 25
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_26_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 26
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_27_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 27
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_28_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 28
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_29_modifier }

				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_30_modifier }
				set_local_variable = {
					name = current_duel_edge_debuff
					value = 30
				}
				#Show a tooltip in case the character is overflowing at max buff.
				custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.tt.maximum_debuff_reached
			}
	}
	# Grab the amount we want to add and change the combat modifier accordingly.
	if = {
		limit = {
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
				POD_damage_spirit_custom_trigger = {
					RECIPIENT = scope:sc_defender
					ACTOR = scope:sc_attacker
				}
			}
			trigger_if = {
				limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
				POD_damage_spirit_custom_trigger = {
					RECIPIENT = scope:sc_attacker
					ACTOR = scope:sc_defender
				}
			}
		}
		change_local_variable = {
			name = current_duel_edge_debuff
			add = $DEBUFF_INCREMENT$
		}
	}
	# Remove any existing duel edge buffs.
	hidden_effect = { remove_temporary_duel_edge_debuff_modifiers_effect = yes }
	# Add the buff corresponding to our new level of edge.
	if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 1 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_01_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 2 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_02_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 3 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_03_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 4 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_04_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 5 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_05_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 6 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_06_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 7 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_07_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 8 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_08_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 9 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_09_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 10 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_10_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 11 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_11_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 12 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_12_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 13 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_13_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 14 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_14_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 15 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_15_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 16 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_16_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 17 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_17_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 18 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_18_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 19 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_19_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 20 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_20_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 21 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_21_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 22 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_22_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 23 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_23_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 24 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_24_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 25 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_25_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 26 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_26_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 27 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_27_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 28 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_28_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff = 29 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_29_modifier
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { local_var:current_duel_edge_debuff >= 30 }
		add_character_modifier = duel_edge_debuff_30_modifier
		#Show a tooltip in case the character is overflowing at max buff.
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.tt.maximum_debuff_reached
	}
	# Finally, note that the character needs a stats recalc.
	add_character_flag = sce_needs_forced_recalc_flag
}

# Select a single POD tier 1 move from the list
POD_select_tier_1_move_effect = {
	random_list = {
		### Vampire ###
	
		# Bite   |note that Bite doesn't become better as tiers go up
		1000 = {
			trigger = {
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_bite_flag }
				POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes
			}
			set_local_variable = {
				name = POD_combat_move_bite_flag
				value = yes
			}
			# Weight down repeat moves
			modifier = {
				add = sce_regular_combat_repeat_down_weight
				POD_repeat_moves_trigger = { MOVE = bite }
			}
		}
		
		# Blood Surge   |note that Blood Surge doesn't become better as tiers go up, it scales with BP
		1000 = {
			trigger = {
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_blood_surge_flag }
				POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes
				NOT = {
					has_character_modifier = bloodpotency0_modifier
				}
				POD_has_enough_blood_for_discipline = { AMOUNT = 1 }
			}
			set_local_variable = {
				name = POD_combat_move_blood_surge_flag
				value = yes
			}
			# Weight down repeat moves
			modifier = {
				add = sce_regular_combat_repeat_down_weight
				POD_repeat_moves_trigger = { MOVE = blood_surge }
			}
		}
		
		# Blood Heal   |note that Blood Heal doesn't become better as tiers go up, it scales with BP
		1000 = {
			trigger = {
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_blood_heal_flag }
				POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes
				OR = {
					has_trait = wounded_1
					has_trait = wounded_2
					has_trait = wounded_3
				}
				POD_has_enough_blood_for_discipline = { AMOUNT = 1 }
			}
			set_local_variable = {
				name = POD_combat_move_blood_heal_flag
				value = yes
			}
			# Weight down repeat moves
			modifier = {
				add = sce_regular_combat_repeat_down_weight
				POD_repeat_moves_trigger = { MOVE = blood_heal }
			}
		}
		
		# Celerity: Blink tier 1
		1000 = {
			trigger = {
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_blink_1_flag }
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_blink_2_flag }
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_blink_3_flag }
				OR = {
					has_perk = blink_perk
					has_trait = celerityadvanced
				}
				POD_has_enough_blood_for_discipline = { AMOUNT = 1 }
			}
			set_local_variable = {
				name = POD_combat_move_blink_1_flag
				value = yes
			}
			# Weight down repeat moves
			modifier = {
				add = sce_regular_combat_repeat_down_weight
				POD_repeat_moves_triple_trigger = { MOVE = blink }
			}
		}
		
		# Celerity: Lightning Strike tier 1
		1000 = {
			trigger = {
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_lightning_strike_1_flag }
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_lightning_strike_2_flag }
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_lightning_strike_3_flag }
				OR = {
					has_perk = lightning_strike_perk
					has_trait = celerityadvanced
				}
				OR = {
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_defender
						scope:sc_attacker = {
							NOT = { has_trait = celerityadvanced }
							NOT = { has_perk = lightning_strike_perk }
						}
					}
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_attacker
						scope:sc_defender = {
							NOT = { has_trait = celerityadvanced }
							NOT = { has_perk = lightning_strike_perk }
						}
					}
				}
				POD_has_enough_blood_for_discipline = { AMOUNT = 1 }
			}
			set_local_variable = {
				name = POD_combat_move_lightning_strike_1_flag
				value = yes
			}
			# Weight down repeat moves
			modifier = {
				add = sce_regular_combat_repeat_down_weight
				POD_repeat_moves_triple_trigger = { MOVE = lightning_strike }
			}
		}

			trigger = {

				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_split_second_2_flag }
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_split_second_3_flag }
				OR = {
					has_perk = split_second_perk
					has_trait = celerityadvanced
				}
				POD_has_enough_blood_for_discipline = { AMOUNT = 1 }
			}
			set_local_variable = {
				name = POD_combat_move_split_second_1_flag

		}

			trigger = {
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_unerring_aim_1_flag }
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_unerring_aim_2_flag }
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_unerring_aim_3_flag }
				has_trait = celerityadvanced
				OR = {
					has_trait = auspex
					has_trait = auspexadvanced
				}
				OR = {
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_attacker
						scope:sc_defender = {
							NOT = { has_trait = celerityadvanced }
						}
					}
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_defender 
						scope:sc_attacker = {
							NOT = { has_trait = celerityadvanced }
						}
					}
				}
				POD_has_enough_blood_for_discipline = { AMOUNT = 1 }
			}
			set_local_variable = {
				name = POD_combat_move_unerring_aim_1_flag

			}

				}
				OR = {
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_attacker

						  NOT = { POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes }
						}
					}
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_defender
						scope:sc_attacker = {
							NOT = { POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes }
						}
					}
				}
				POD_has_enough_blood_for_discipline = { AMOUNT = 1 }

				OR = {
					has_perk = brutal_feed_perk
					has_trait = potenceadvanced
				}
				NOR = {
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_defender
						scope:sc_attacker = {
							OR = {
								has_trait = truefaith
								has_trait = beatific
								has_trait = orison_sanguine
							}
						}
					}
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_attacker
						scope:sc_defender = {
							OR = {
								has_trait = truefaith
								has_trait = beatific
								has_trait = orison_sanguine
							}
						}
					}
				}
				OR = {
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_defender
						POD_eat_spirit_custom_trigger = {
							RECIPIENT = scope:sc_attacker
							ACTOR = scope:sc_defender
						}
					}
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_attacker
						POD_eat_spirit_custom_trigger = {
							RECIPIENT = scope:sc_defender
							ACTOR = scope:sc_attacker
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_local_variable = {
				name = POD_combat_move_brutal_feed_1_flag
				value = yes
			}

							NOT = { has_perk = sense_the_unseen_perk }
							NOT = { has_trait = auspexadvanced }
							NOT = { has_perk = oculi_sight_perk }
						}
					}
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_defender 
						scope:sc_attacker = {
							NOT = { has_perk = sense_the_unseen_perk }
							NOT = { has_trait = auspexadvanced }
							NOT = { has_perk = oculi_sight_perk }
						}
					}
				}
				POD_has_enough_blood_for_discipline = { AMOUNT = 1 }
			}
			set_local_variable = {
				name = POD_combat_move_vanish_1_flag
				value = yes
			}
			# Weight down repeat moves
			modifier = {
				add = sce_regular_combat_repeat_down_weight
				POD_repeat_moves_triple_trigger = { MOVE = vanish }
			}
		}

						scope:sc_defender = {
							OR = {
								has_trait = truefaith
								has_trait = beatific
								has_trait = orison_sanguine
							}
						}
					}
				}
				OR = {
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_defender
						POD_eat_spirit_custom_trigger = {
							RECIPIENT = scope:sc_attacker
							ACTOR = scope:sc_defender
						}
					}
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_attacker
						POD_eat_spirit_custom_trigger = {
							RECIPIENT = scope:sc_defender
							ACTOR = scope:sc_attacker
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_local_variable = {
				name = POD_combat_move_typhonic_maw_1_flag
				value = yes
			}
			# Weight down repeat moves
			modifier = {
				add = sce_regular_combat_repeat_down_weight
				POD_repeat_moves_triple_trigger = { MOVE = typhonic_maw }
			}
		}

		# Use a Torch  -  usable by everyone, but vampires
		1000 = {
			trigger = {
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_fire_torch_1_flag }
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_fire_torch_2_flag }
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_fire_torch_3_flag }
				OR = {
					is_supehunter_trigger = yes
					is_mortal_trigger = yes
					is_ghoul_trigger = yes
					is_revenant_trigger = yes
				}
				OR = {
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_attacker 
						scope:sc_defender = {
							POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_defender
						scope:sc_attacker = {
						   POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = firebombs_modifier }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = firespewer_modifier }
			}
			set_local_variable = {
				name = POD_combat_move_fire_torch_1_flag
				value = yes
			}

			modifier = {
				add = sce_regular_combat_repeat_down_weight

# Select a single POD tier 2 move from the list
POD_select_tier_2_move_effect = {
	random_list = {
		### Vampire ###
	
		# Bite   |note that Bite doesn't become better as tiers go up
		1000 = {
			trigger = {
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_bite_flag }
				POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes
			}
			set_local_variable = {
				name = POD_combat_move_bite_flag
				value = yes
			}
			# Weight down repeat moves
			modifier = {
				add = sce_regular_combat_repeat_down_weight
				POD_repeat_moves_trigger = { MOVE = bite }
			}
		}
		
		# Blood Surge   |note that Blood Surge doesn't become better as tiers go up, it scales with BP
		1000 = {
			trigger = {
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_blood_surge_flag }
				POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes
				NOT = {
					has_character_modifier = bloodpotency0_modifier
				}
				POD_has_enough_blood_for_discipline = { AMOUNT = 1 }
			}
			set_local_variable = {
				name = POD_combat_move_blood_surge_flag
				value = yes
			}
			# Weight down repeat moves
			modifier = {
				add = sce_regular_combat_repeat_down_weight
				POD_repeat_moves_trigger = { MOVE = blood_surge }
			}
		}

					has_trait = wounded_1
					has_trait = wounded_2
					has_trait = wounded_3

				POD_has_enough_blood_for_discipline = { AMOUNT = 1 }
			}
			set_local_variable = {
				name = POD_combat_move_blood_heal_flag

		#Serpentis: Typhonic Maw tier 2
		1000 = {
			trigger = {
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_typhonic_maw_1_flag }
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_typhonic_maw_2_flag }
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_typhonic_maw_3_flag }
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_ai = yes
						OR = {
							has_trait = serpentis
							has_trait = serpentisadvanced
						}
					}							
					AND = {
						is_ai = no
						OR = {
							has_perk = typhonic_maw_perk
							has_trait = serpentisadvanced
						}
					}
				}
				POD_has_enough_blood_for_discipline = { AMOUNT = 1 }
				NOR = {
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_defender
						scope:sc_attacker = {
							OR = {
								has_trait = truefaith
								has_trait = beatific
								has_trait = orison_sanguine
							}
						}
					}
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_attacker
						scope:sc_defender = {
							OR = {
								has_trait = truefaith
								has_trait = beatific
								has_trait = orison_sanguine
							}
						}
					}
				}
				OR = {
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_defender
						POD_eat_spirit_custom_trigger = {
							RECIPIENT = scope:sc_attacker
							ACTOR = scope:sc_defender
						}
					}
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_attacker
						POD_eat_spirit_custom_trigger = {
							RECIPIENT = scope:sc_defender
							ACTOR = scope:sc_attacker
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_local_variable = {
				name = POD_combat_move_typhonic_maw_2_flag
				value = yes
			}
			# Weight down repeat moves
			modifier = {
				add = sce_regular_combat_repeat_down_weight

					is_mortal_trigger = yes
					is_ghoul_trigger = yes
					is_revenant_trigger = yes
				}
				OR = {
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_attacker

							POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_defender
						scope:sc_attacker = {
						   POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = firebombs_modifier }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = firespewer_modifier }
			}

				name = POD_combat_move_fire_torch_2_flag

				POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes
				NOT = {
					has_character_modifier = bloodpotency0_modifier

			}

		#Oblivion: Touch of Oblivion tier 3
		1000 = {
			trigger = {
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_touch_of_oblivion_1_flag }
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_touch_of_oblivion_2_flag }
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_touch_of_oblivion_3_flag }
				OR = {
					AND = {
						is_ai = yes
						OR = {
							has_trait = oblivion
							has_trait = oblivionadvanced
						}
					}							
					AND = {
						is_ai = no
						has_trait = oblivionadvanced
					}
				}
				POD_has_enough_blood_for_discipline = { AMOUNT = 1 }
			}
			set_local_variable = {
				name = POD_combat_move_touch_of_oblivion_3_flag
				value = yes
			}
			# Weight down repeat moves
			modifier = {
				add = sce_regular_combat_repeat_down_weight
				POD_repeat_moves_triple_trigger = { MOVE = touch_of_oblivion }
			}
		}

		### Hunters ###

		# Use a Torch tier 3 -  usable by everyone, but vampires
		1000 = {
			trigger = {
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_fire_torch_1_flag }
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_fire_torch_2_flag }
				NOT = { exists = local_var:POD_combat_move_fire_torch_3_flag }
				OR = {
					is_supehunter_trigger = yes
					is_mortal_trigger = yes
					is_ghoul_trigger = yes
					is_revenant_trigger = yes
				}
				OR = {
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_attacker 
						scope:sc_defender = {
							POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes
						}
					}
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_defender
						scope:sc_attacker = {
						   POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes
						}
					}
				}
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = firebombs_modifier }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = firespewer_modifier }
			}
			set_local_variable = {
				name = POD_combat_move_fire_torch_3_flag
				value = yes
			}
			# Weight down repeat moves
			modifier = {
				add = sce_regular_combat_repeat_down_weight
				POD_repeat_moves_triple_trigger = { MOVE = fire_torch }
			}
		}

							has_trait = serpentisadvanced
						}
					}
				}
				POD_has_enough_blood_for_discipline = { AMOUNT = 1 }
				NOR = {
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_defender
						scope:sc_attacker = {
							OR = {
								has_trait = truefaith
								has_trait = beatific
								has_trait = orison_sanguine
							}
						}
					}
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_attacker
						scope:sc_defender = {

								has_trait = truefaith

							}
						}
					}
				}
				OR = {
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_defender
						POD_eat_spirit_custom_trigger = {
							RECIPIENT = scope:sc_attacker
							ACTOR = scope:sc_defender
						}
					}
					AND = {
						this = scope:sc_attacker
						POD_eat_spirit_custom_trigger = {
							RECIPIENT = scope:sc_defender
							ACTOR = scope:sc_attacker
						}
					}
				}

POD_save_last_move_effect = {
	#Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = sc_attacker_last_move
			value = flag:POD_$MOVE$
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		save_scope_value_as = {
			name = sc_defender_last_move
			value = flag:POD_$MOVE$
		}
	}
}

#POD MOVES TIER 1

# Bite
POD_combat_move_bite_effect = {
	#Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
	POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = bite }

	#And adjust risk-reward scores.
	if = {
		limit = { has_perk = ns_kiss_of_the_dark_mother_perk }
		adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
			INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_medium
			DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_medium
		}
	}
	else = {
		adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
			INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_medium
			DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_low
		}
	}
}

# Blood Surge
POD_combat_move_blood_surge_effect = {
    #Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
	POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = blood_surge }
	
    #Buff duel edge depending on character's Blood Potency
	if = {
	    limit = {
		    OR = {
			    has_character_modifier = bloodpotency10_modifier
				has_character_modifier = bloodpotency9_modifier
			}
		}
		increment_combat_buffs_effect = { BUFF_INCREMENT = 5 }
	}
	if = {
	    limit = {
		    OR = {
			    has_character_modifier = bloodpotency8_modifier
				has_character_modifier = bloodpotency7_modifier
			}
		}
		increment_combat_buffs_effect = { BUFF_INCREMENT = 4 }
	}
	if = {
	    limit = {
		    OR = {
			    has_character_modifier = bloodpotency6_modifier
				has_character_modifier = bloodpotency5_modifier
			}
		}
		increment_combat_buffs_effect = { BUFF_INCREMENT = 3 }
	}
	if = {
	    limit = {
		    OR = {
			    has_character_modifier = bloodpotency4_modifier
				has_character_modifier = bloodpotency3_modifier
			}
		}
		increment_combat_buffs_effect = { BUFF_INCREMENT = 2 }
	}
	if = {
	    limit = {
		    OR = {
			    has_character_modifier = bloodpotency2_modifier
				has_character_modifier = bloodpotency1_modifier
			}
		}
		increment_combat_buffs_effect = { BUFF_INCREMENT = 1 }
	}
	
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_none
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_none
	}
}

# Blood Heal
POD_combat_move_blood_heal_effect = {
    #Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
	POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = blood_heal }
	
	POD_blood_heal_wound_effect = yes
	
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_none
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_none
	}
}

# Celerity: Lightning Strike
POD_combat_move_lightning_strike_1_effect = {
	#Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
    POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = lightning_strike_1 }
	
	# Reduce your opponent's wound threshold by making an unresisted attack
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_defender_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_1
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.minor_malus.tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_attacker_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_1
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.minor_malus.tt
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_low
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_high
	}
	
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
}

# Potence: Brutal Feed
POD_combat_move_brutal_feed_1_effect = {
    #Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
    POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = brutal_feed_1 }
	
	if = {
	    limit = {
		   this = scope:sc_attacker
		}
		#Take a sip
		POD_drink_from_victim_effect = {
			AMOUNT = 1
			VICTIM = scope:sc_defender
		}
		 scope:sc_defender = {
		     increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 2 }
		 }
	}
	if = {
	    limit = {
		   this = scope:sc_defender
		}
		#Take a sip
		POD_drink_from_victim_effect = {
			AMOUNT = 1
			VICTIM = scope:sc_attacker
		}
		 scope:sc_attacker = {
		     increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 2 }
		 }
	}

	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_medium
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_low
	}
}

# Potence: Earthshock
POD_combat_move_earthshock_1_effect = {
    #Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
    POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = earthshock_1 }
	
	# Reduce your opponent's wound threshold and duel edge by throwing your opponent off-balance
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_defender_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_1
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.minor_malus.tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_attacker_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_1
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.minor_malus.tt
	}
	
	
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_medium
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_high
	}
	# Make two rouse checks
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
}

	}
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
}


# Fortitude: Prowess from Pain
POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_1_effect = {
    #Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
    POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = prowess_from_pain_1 }
	
	if = {
		limit = {
			# To cancel out wounded, they must have any wounded trait.
			has_trait = wounded
			# And must not have cancelled it out already.
			NOR = {
				has_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_1_modifier
				has_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_2_modifier
				has_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_3_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = wounded_1 }
			add_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_1_modifier
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_trait = wounded_2 }
			add_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_1_modifier
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_trait = wounded_3 }
			add_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_1_modifier
		}
	}
	else = {
		# Otherwise, we just buff their duel edge twice.
		increment_combat_buffs_effect = { BUFF_INCREMENT = 2 }
	}
	
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_medium
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_high
	}
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
}

    #Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
    POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = nocturne_1 }
	
	if = {
	     limit = {
		     this = scope:sc_attacker
		 }
		 #Take a sip
		POD_drink_from_victim_effect = {
			AMOUNT = 1
			VICTIM = scope:sc_defender
		}
		 scope:sc_defender = {
		     increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 1 }
		 }
	}
	if = {
	     limit = {
		     this = scope:sc_defender
		 }
		 #Take a sip
		POD_drink_from_victim_effect = {
			AMOUNT = 1
			VICTIM = scope:sc_attacker
		}
		 scope:sc_attacker = {
		     increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 1 }
		 }
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_low
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_low
	}

	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes

# Daimonion: Adramelechs Wrath
POD_combat_move_adramelechs_wrath_1_effect = {
    #Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
    POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = adramelechs_wrath_1 }
	
	#Add duel edge add_stress = major_stress_gain
	increment_combat_buffs_effect = { BUFF_INCREMENT = 2 }
	
	random_list = {
	    50 = {
		   if = {
		        limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = sc_finished
					value = yes
				}
				scope:sc_attacker = { save_scope_as = sc_victor }
				scope:sc_defender = { save_scope_as = sc_loser }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = victory_type
					value = flag:sudden_death
				}
				# Error: has_variable trigger [ This scope doesn't support variables.
				show_as_tooltip = {
					scope:sc_defender = {
					   death = {
						   death_reason = death_duel
						   killer = scope:sc_attacker
					   }
					}
				}
	        }
	        if = {
		        limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = sc_finished
					value = yes
				}
				scope:sc_defender = { save_scope_as = sc_victor }
				scope:sc_attacker = { save_scope_as = sc_loser }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = victory_type
					value = flag:sudden_death
				}
				# Error: has_variable trigger [ This scope doesn't support variables. 
				show_as_tooltip = {
					scope:sc_attacker = {
					   death = {
						   death_reason = death_duel
						   killer = scope:sc_defender
					   }
					}
				}
	        }
		}
		
		25 = {
		    if = {
		        limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = sc_finished
					value = yes
				}
				scope:sc_defender = { save_scope_as = sc_victor }
				scope:sc_attacker = { save_scope_as = sc_loser }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = victory_type
					value = flag:sudden_death
				}
				# Error: has_variable trigger [ This scope doesn't support variables. 
				show_as_tooltip = {
					scope:sc_attacker = {
					   death = {
						   death_reason = death_suicide
					   }
					}
				}
	        }
	        if = {
		        limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = sc_finished
					value = yes
				}
				scope:sc_attacker = { save_scope_as = sc_victor }
				scope:sc_defender = { save_scope_as = sc_loser }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = victory_type
					value = flag:sudden_death
				}
				# Error: has_variable trigger [ This scope doesn't support variables. 
				show_as_tooltip = {
					scope:sc_defender = {
					   death = {
						   death_reason = death_suicide
					   }
					}
				}
	        }
		}
		
		25 = {
		
		}
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_very_high
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_low
	}
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
}

			}

# Fire Torch
POD_combat_move_fire_torch_1_effect = {
    POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = fire_torch_1 }
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
			if = {
				limit = { POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes }
				add_stress = medium_stress_gain
			}
			else = { add_stress = minor_stress_gain }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
			if = {
				limit = { POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes }
				add_stress = medium_stress_gain
			}
			else = { add_stress = minor_stress_gain }
		}
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_medium
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_low
	}
}

			}

		}

	else_if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }

			if = {

			}
			else = { add_stress = minor_stress_gain }
		}
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_medium
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_medium
	}
}

# Fire Spewer
POD_combat_move_fire_spewer_1_effect = {
    POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = fire_spewer_1 }
	
	# Reduce your opponent's wound threshold and duel edge, because fire
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_defender_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_2
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.medium_malus.tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_attacker_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_2
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.medium_malus.tt
	}
	
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
			if = {
				limit = { POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes }
				add_stress = medium_stress_gain
			}
			else = { add_stress = minor_stress_gain }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
			if = {
				limit = { POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes }
				add_stress = medium_stress_gain
			}
			else = { add_stress = minor_stress_gain }
		}
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_very_high
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_high
	}
}

# Stake Attack
POD_combat_move_stake_attack_1_effect = {
    POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = stake_attack_1 }
	
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					this = scope:sc_attacker
					scope:sc_defender = { POD_immune_to_staking_trigger = no }
				}
				AND = {
					this = scope:sc_defender
					scope:sc_attacker = { POD_immune_to_staking_trigger = no }
				}
			}
		}
		adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
			INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_high
			DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_medium
		}
	}
	else = {
		adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
			INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_high
			DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_none
		}
	}
}

	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_medium
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_medium
	}
	POD_splat_agnostic_energy_cost = { COUNT = 1 }
}

# Celerity: Lightning Strike
POD_combat_move_lightning_strike_2_effect = {
	#Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
    POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = lightning_strike_2 }
	
	# Reduce your opponent's wound threshold by making an unresisted attack
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_defender_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_1
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.minor_malus.tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_attacker_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_1
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.minor_malus.tt
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_low
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_very_high
	}
	
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
}

# Fortitude: Prowess from Pain
POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_2_effect = {
    #Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
    POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = prowess_from_pain_2 }
	
	if = {
		limit = {
			# To cancel out wounded, they must have any wounded trait.
			has_trait = wounded
			# And must not have cancelled it out already.
			NOR = {
				has_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_1_modifier
				has_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_2_modifier
				has_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_3_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = wounded_1 }
			add_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_1_modifier
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_trait = wounded_2 }
			add_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_1_modifier
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_trait = wounded_3 }
			add_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_1_modifier
		}
	}
	else = {
		# Otherwise, we just buff their duel edge twice.
		increment_combat_buffs_effect = { BUFF_INCREMENT = 3 }
	}
	
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_medium
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_very_high
	}
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
}

	# Debuff enemy duel edge by exploding their blood a little bit
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
		    increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 4 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
		    increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 4 }
		}
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_low
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_very_high
	}
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
	add_piety = medium_piety_loss
}

# Oblivion: Stygian Shroud
POD_combat_move_stygian_shroud_2_effect = {
    #Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
    POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = stygian_shroud_2 }
	
	# Reduce your opponent's wound threshold and duel edge by blinding opponent
	if = {
		limit = {
		    this = scope:sc_attacker
			scope:sc_defender = {
			   NOR = {
					has_perk = eyes_of_the_abyss_perk
					has_trait = obtenebrationadvanced
					has_perk = oculi_sight_perk
			   }
			}
		}
		scope:sc_defender = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_defender_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_2
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.medium_malus.tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 
		    this = scope:sc_defender
			scope:sc_attacker = {
			   NOR = {
					has_perk = eyes_of_the_abyss_perk
					has_trait = obtenebrationadvanced
					has_perk = oculi_sight_perk
			   }
			}
		}
		scope:sc_attacker = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_attacker_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_2
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.medium_malus.tt
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_low
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_medium
	}
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
}

	     limit = {
		     this = scope:sc_attacker
		 }
		#Steal enemies blood
		POD_drink_from_victim_effect = {
			AMOUNT = 2
			VICTIM = scope:sc_defender
		}
	}
	if = {
	     limit = {
		     this = scope:sc_defender
		}
		POD_drink_from_victim_effect = {
			AMOUNT = 2
			VICTIM = scope:sc_attacker
		}
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {

# Daimonion: Adramelechs Wrath
POD_combat_move_adramelechs_wrath_2_effect = {
    #Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
    POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = adramelechs_wrath_2 }
	
	#Add duel edge add_stress = major_stress_gain
	increment_combat_buffs_effect = { BUFF_INCREMENT = 3 }
	
	random_list = {
	    50 = {
		   if = {
		        limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = sc_finished
					value = yes
				}
				scope:sc_attacker = { save_scope_as = sc_victor }
				scope:sc_defender = { save_scope_as = sc_loser }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = victory_type
					value = flag:sudden_death
				}
				# Error: has_variable trigger [ This scope doesn't support variables.
				show_as_tooltip = {
					scope:sc_defender = {
					   death = {
						   death_reason = death_duel
						   killer = scope:sc_attacker
					   }
					}
				}
	        }
	        if = {
		        limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = sc_finished
					value = yes
				}
				scope:sc_defender = { save_scope_as = sc_victor }
				scope:sc_attacker = { save_scope_as = sc_loser }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = victory_type
					value = flag:sudden_death
				}
				# Error: has_variable trigger [ This scope doesn't support variables. 
				show_as_tooltip = {
					scope:sc_attacker = {
					   death = {
						   death_reason = death_duel
						   killer = scope:sc_defender
					   }
					}
				}
	        }
		}
		
		25 = {
		    if = {
		        limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = sc_finished
					value = yes
				}
				scope:sc_defender = { save_scope_as = sc_victor }
				scope:sc_attacker = { save_scope_as = sc_loser }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = victory_type
					value = flag:sudden_death
				}
				# Error: has_variable trigger [ This scope doesn't support variables. 
				show_as_tooltip = {
					scope:sc_attacker = {
					   death = {
						   death_reason = death_suicide
					   }
					}
				}
	        }
	        if = {
		        limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = sc_finished
					value = yes
				}
				scope:sc_attacker = { save_scope_as = sc_victor }
				scope:sc_defender = { save_scope_as = sc_loser }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = victory_type
					value = flag:sudden_death
				}
				# Error: has_variable trigger [ This scope doesn't support variables. 
				show_as_tooltip = {
					scope:sc_defender = {
					   death = {
						   death_reason = death_suicide
					   }
					}
				}
	        }
		}
		
		25 = {
		
		}
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_very_high
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_medium
	}
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
}

# Necromancy: Ashen Lady's Embrace

		limit = { has_perk = ns_dust_to_dust_perk }

		if = {
			limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
			scope:sc_defender = {
				increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 4 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
			scope:sc_attacker = {
				increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 4 }
			}
		}
		
		adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
			INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_low
			DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_medium
		}
	}
	else = {
		# Debuff enemy duel edge
		if = {
			limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
			scope:sc_defender = {
				increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 6 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
			scope:sc_attacker = {
				increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 6 }
			}
		}
		
		adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
			INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_none
			DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_high
		}
	}
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
}

# Fire Torch
POD_combat_move_fire_torch_2_effect = {
    POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = fire_torch_2 }
	
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
		    if = {
		        limit = { POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes }
				add_stress = minor_stress_gain
		    }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
		    if = {
			    limit = { POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes }
				add_stress = minor_stress_gain
			}
		}
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_medium
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_medium
	}
}

# Fire Bombs
POD_combat_move_fire_bombs_2_effect = {
    POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = fire_bombs_2 }
	
	# Reduce your opponent's wound threshold and duel edge, because fire
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_defender_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_2
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.medium_malus.tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_attacker_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_2
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.medium_malus.tt
	}
	
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
			if = {
				limit = { POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes }
				add_stress = medium_stress_gain
			}
			else = { add_stress = minor_stress_gain }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
			if = {
				limit = { POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes }
				add_stress = medium_stress_gain
			}
			else = { add_stress = minor_stress_gain }
		}
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_low
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_medium
	}
}

		}

				add_stress = major_stress_gain
			}
			else = { add_stress = medium_stress_gain }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
			if = {
				limit = { POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes }
				add_stress = major_stress_gain
			}
			else = { add_stress = medium_stress_gain }
		}
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_high
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_high
	}
}

# Stake Attack
POD_combat_move_stake_attack_2_effect = {
    POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = stake_attack_2 }
	
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					this = scope:sc_attacker
					scope:sc_defender = { POD_immune_to_staking_trigger = no }
				}
				AND = {
					this = scope:sc_defender
					scope:sc_attacker = { POD_immune_to_staking_trigger = no }
				}
			}
		}
		adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
			INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_high
			DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_high
		}
	}
	else = {
		adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
			INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_high
			DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_none
		}
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		change_variable = {
			name = sc_attacker_injury_bonus
			add = combat_move_wound_threshold_bonus_2
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.medium_bonus.tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		change_variable = {
			name = sc_defender_injury_bonus
			add = combat_move_wound_threshold_bonus_2
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.medium_bonus.tt
	}

	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_medium
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_medium
	}
	POD_splat_agnostic_energy_cost = { COUNT = 2 }
}

# Ghost-Flame Shintai: Goblin Scorch
POD_combat_move_goblin_scorch_effect = {
    POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = goblin_scorch }
	
	# Reduce your opponent's wound threshold and duel edge, because fire
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_defender_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_2
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.medium_malus.tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_attacker_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_2
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.medium_malus.tt
	}
	
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
		scope:sc_defender = {
			if = {
				limit = { POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes }
				add_stress = medium_stress_gain
			}
			else = { add_stress = minor_stress_gain }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		scope:sc_attacker = {
			if = {
				limit = { POD_is_vampire_or_abomination_trigger = yes }
				add_stress = medium_stress_gain
			}
			else = { add_stress = minor_stress_gain }
		}
	}
	
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_low
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_high
	}
	POD_kj_art_cost_effect = { COUNT = 2 }
}

			add = combat_move_wound_threshold_bonus_2
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.medium_bonus.tt
	}
	if = {
		limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
		change_variable = {
			name = sc_defender_injury_bonus
			add = combat_move_wound_threshold_bonus_2
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.medium_bonus.tt
	}
	
	#Add duel edge for having more HP
	increment_combat_buffs_effect = { BUFF_INCREMENT = 5 }
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_none
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_medium
	}
}

			add = combat_move_wound_threshold_bonus_3
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.major_bonus.tt
	}
	
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {

		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_very_high
	}
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
}


# Fortitude: Prowess from Pain
POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_3_effect = {
    #Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
    POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = prowess_from_pain_3 }
	
	if = {
		limit = {
			# To cancel out wounded, they must have any wounded trait.
			has_trait = wounded
			# And must not have cancelled it out already.
			NOR = {
				has_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_1_modifier
				has_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_2_modifier
				has_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_3_modifier
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_trait = wounded_1 }
			add_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_1_modifier
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_trait = wounded_2 }
			add_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_1_modifier
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_trait = wounded_3 }
			add_character_modifier = POD_combat_move_prowess_from_pain_wounded_1_modifier
		}
	}
	else = {
		# Otherwise, we just buff their duel edge twice.
		increment_combat_buffs_effect = { BUFF_INCREMENT = 4 }
	}
	
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_low
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_very_high
	}
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
}

# Obtenebration: Nocturne
POD_combat_move_nocturne_3_effect = {
    #Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
    POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = nocturne_3 }

	if = {
	     limit = {
		    this = scope:sc_attacker
		 }
		 #Take a drink
		POD_drink_from_victim_effect = {
			AMOUNT = 2
			VICTIM = scope:sc_defender
		}
		 scope:sc_defender = {
		     increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 2 }
		 }
	}
	if = {
	     limit = {
		    this = scope:sc_defender
		 }
		 #Take a drink
		POD_drink_from_victim_effect = {
			AMOUNT = 2
			VICTIM = scope:sc_attacker
		}
		 scope:sc_attacker = {
		     increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 2 }
		 }
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_low
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_high
	}
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
}

		    this = scope:sc_defender

					has_trait = obtenebrationadvanced

			   }
			}
		}
		scope:sc_attacker = {
			change_variable = {
				name = sc_attacker_injury_risk_score
				add = combat_move_wound_threshold_malus_3
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = single_combat.0001.wound_threshold.major_malus.tt
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {

# Daimonion: Adramelechs Wrath
POD_combat_move_adramelechs_wrath_3_effect = {
    #Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
    POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = adramelechs_wrath_3 }
	
	#Add duel edge add_stress = major_stress_gain
	increment_combat_buffs_effect = { BUFF_INCREMENT = 4 }
	
	random_list = {
	    50 = {
		   if = {
		        limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = sc_finished
					value = yes
				}
				scope:sc_attacker = { save_scope_as = sc_victor }
				scope:sc_defender = { save_scope_as = sc_loser }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = victory_type
					value = flag:sudden_death
				}
				# Error: has_variable trigger [ This scope doesn't support variables.
				show_as_tooltip = {
					scope:sc_defender = {
					   death = {
						   death_reason = death_duel
						   killer = scope:sc_attacker
					   }
					}
				}
	        }
	        if = {
		        limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = sc_finished
					value = yes
				}
				scope:sc_defender = { save_scope_as = sc_victor }
				scope:sc_attacker = { save_scope_as = sc_loser }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = victory_type
					value = flag:sudden_death
				}
				# Error: has_variable trigger [ This scope doesn't support variables. 
				show_as_tooltip = {
					scope:sc_attacker = {
					   death = {
						   death_reason = death_duel
						   killer = scope:sc_defender
					   }
					}
				}
	        }
		}
		
		25 = {
		    if = {
		        limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = sc_finished
					value = yes
				}
				scope:sc_defender = { save_scope_as = sc_victor }
				scope:sc_attacker = { save_scope_as = sc_loser }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = victory_type
					value = flag:sudden_death
				}
				# Error: has_variable trigger [ This scope doesn't support variables. 
				show_as_tooltip = {
					scope:sc_attacker = {
					   death = {
						   death_reason = death_suicide
					   }
					}
				}
	        }
	        if = {
		        limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = sc_finished
					value = yes
				}
				scope:sc_attacker = { save_scope_as = sc_victor }
				scope:sc_defender = { save_scope_as = sc_loser }
				save_scope_value_as = {
					name = victory_type
					value = flag:sudden_death
				}
				# Error: has_variable trigger [ This scope doesn't support variables. 
				show_as_tooltip = {
					scope:sc_defender = {
					   death = {
						   death_reason = death_suicide
					   }
					}
				}
	        }
		}
		
		25 = {
		
		}
	}
	
	adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
		INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_high
		DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_high
	}
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
}

# Necromancy: Ashen Lady's Embrace
POD_combat_move_ashen_ladys_embrace_3_effect = {
    #Change the last move registered for loc purposes.
    POD_save_last_move_effect = { MOVE = ashen_ladys_embrace_3 }
	
	if = {
		limit = { has_perk = ns_dust_to_dust_perk }

		if = {
			limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
			scope:sc_defender = {
				increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 6 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
			scope:sc_attacker = {
				increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 6 }
			}
		}
		
		adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
			INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_none
			DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_high
		}
	}
	else = {
		# Debuff enemy duel edge
		if = {
			limit = { this = scope:sc_attacker }
			scope:sc_defender = {
				increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 8 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { this = scope:sc_defender }
			scope:sc_attacker = {
				increment_combat_debuffs_effect = { DEBUFF_INCREMENT = 8 }
			}
		}
		
		adjust_risk_reward_effect = {
			INJURY_RISK = injury_risk_none
			DUEL_SUCCESS = duel_success_very_high
		}
	}
	# Make a rouse check
	POD_hunger_rouse_check_effect = yes
}
